    comments: 'Margarete Kartäuserin, Nuremberg, Unclear, Medium, Nuremberg, Stadtbibliothek,
      Cent. VI 43y, • The main section was written by a single hand: Margareta, the
      Carthusian (died 1489) (referenced in Würfel, p. 46, and MBK III, 3, p. 613).
      • Additions by several other hands until around 1500. • Nuremberg dialect with
      slight Alemannic influences (as Margareta had spent 1422–28 in Schönensteinbach,
      Alsace).'
  production_unit:
